is the same. We may thus define

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{Z}

\displaystyle=\EV_{P\sim\mathbb{P}_{Z}}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}|{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}\rangle\!\langle{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}

(218)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{\rm even}

\displaystyle=\EV_{P\sim\mathbb{P}_{\rm even}}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}|{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}\rangle\!\langle{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}

(219)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{\rm odd}

\displaystyle=\EV_{P\sim\mathbb{P}_{\rm odd}}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}|{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}\rangle\!\langle{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}

(220)

and write

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}_{\rm twirl}

\displaystyle=\chi^{\prime}_{0}|{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}\rangle\!\langle{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}|+\big[\chi^{\prime}_{Z}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{Z}\big]+\big[\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{\rm even}\big]+\big[\chi^{\prime}_{\rm odd}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{\rm odd}\big]

(221)

Next, we explicitly compute the term of
Z
-like Paulis.

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{Z}=\frac{1}{2^{n}-1}\sum_{0^{n}\neq u\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}^{u}|{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}\rangle\!\langle{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}^{u}=\frac{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}}{2^{n}-1}-\frac{|{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}\rangle\!\langle{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}|}{2^{n}-1}\,,

(222)

which follows since the set of
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}^{u}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}\rangle
form an orthonormal basis for
u\in\{0,1\}^{n}
, and sampling a random vector from an orthonormal basis yields the maximally mixed state.

Additionally, we can use the fact that the Pauli matrices form a 1-design
[
129
]
to say that if we draw a
P
uniformly at random from all of
\mathbb{P}
, conjugating an arbitrary state
|{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}}\rangle\!\langle{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}}|
by
P
yields the maximally mixed state after averaging over
P
. Weighting the different subsets by their sizes (e.g., a
(2^{n}-1)/2^{2n}
fraction of all signed Pauli strings are in
\mathbb{P}_{Z}
), this fact is equivalent to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{2n}}|{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}}\rangle\!\langle{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}}|+\frac{2^{n}-1}{2^{2n}}\EV_{P\in\mathbb{P}_{Z}}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}|{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}}\rangle\!\langle{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}+\frac{2^{n}-1}{2^{n+1}}\EV_{P\in\mathbb{P}_{\rm even}}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}|{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}}\rangle\!\langle{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}+\frac{2^{n}-1}{2^{n+1}}\EV_{P\in\mathbb{P}_{\rm odd}}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}|{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}}\rangle\!\langle{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\xi$}}\smash[t]{\xi}}}}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}=\frac{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}}{2^{n}}

(223)

We apply this facts to the state
|{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}\rangle\!\langle{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}|
, substitute
Eq.

222
, and rearrange to write

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{\rm even}=2\frac{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}}{2^{n}}-\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{\rm odd}

(224)

Next, we plug this into
Eq.

221
to get rid of
\rho(g)_{\rm even}
and say

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}_{\rm twirl}

\displaystyle=(\chi^{\prime}_{0}-\frac{\chi^{\prime}_{Z}}{2^{n}-1})|{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}\rangle\!\langle{\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}}|+\big[\big(\frac{2^{n}}{2^{n}-1}\chi^{\prime}_{Z}+2\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even}\big)\frac{\mathbb{I}}{2^{n}}\big]+\big[(\chi^{\prime}_{\rm odd}-\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even})\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{\rm odd}\big]

(225)

We are now ready to conclude. A consequence of
Proposition

14
is that
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{\rm odd}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}\rangle=0
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}_{\rm twirl}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}\rangle=\lambda_{\rm twirl}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}\rangle

(226)

with
\lambda_{\rm twirl}=\chi_{0}^{\prime}+2^{-n+1}\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even}
. From the definition of
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}_{\rm twirl}
and the relation in
Proposition

3
, we have

\displaystyle\lambda_{\rm twirl}=\langle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}_{\rm twirl}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}\rangle=\EV_{C\sim\mathbb{T}}\langle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$C$}}\smash[t]{C}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$C$}}\smash[t]{C}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$C$}}\smash[t]{C}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$C$}}\smash[t]{C}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g_{C})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g_{C})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\phi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g_{C})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g_{C})}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$C$}}\smash[t]{C}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$C$}}\smash[t]{C}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$C$}}\smash[t]{C}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$C$}}\smash[t]{C}}}^{\dagger}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g)}}}\rangle=\EV_{C\sim\mathbb{T}}\langle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g_{C})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g_{C})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g_{C})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g_{C})}}}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g_{C})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g_{C})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\phi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g_{C})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g_{C})}}}^{\dagger}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g_{C})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g_{C})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\Psi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g_{C})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\Psi(g_{C})$}}\smash[t]{\Psi(g_{C})}}}\rangle\geq F_{\rm min}\,.

(227)

Finally, we reason about the other eigenvalues of
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}_{\rm twirl}
. We may note from their definitions in
Eq.

219
and
Eq.

220
that
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{\rm even}
and
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{\rm odd}
are positive semidefinite operators. From
Eq.

224
, this means the eigenvalues of
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{\rm odd}
cannot exceed
2^{-n+1}
. We now turn back to
Eq.

225
to bound any other eigenvalue
\lambda_{\rm other}
of an eigenvector
|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}\rangle
of
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}_{\rm twirl}
. We have

\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\phi(g)$}}\smash[t]{\phi(g)}}}_{\rm twirl}|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}\rangle=\big[\big(\frac{2^{n}}{2^{n}-1}\chi^{\prime}_{Z}+2\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even}\big)\frac{1}{2^{n}}\big]|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}\rangle+\big[(\chi^{\prime}_{\rm odd}-\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even})\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{\rm odd}\big]|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}\rangle\,.

(228)

Thus,
|\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\lambda_{\rm other}$}}\smash[t]{\lambda_{\rm other}}}}\rangle
must also be an eigenvector of
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\rho(g)$}}\smash[t]{\rho(g)}}}_{\rm odd}
; assume the associated eigenvalue is
C\geq 0
. We have,

\displaystyle\lambda_{\rm other}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{2^{n}}{2^{n}-1}\chi^{\prime}_{Z}+2\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even}\right)\frac{1}{2^{n}}+(\chi^{\prime}_{\rm odd}-\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even})C

(229)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2^{n}-1}\chi^{\prime}_{Z}+\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even}\left(\frac{1}{2^{n-1}}-C\right)+\chi^{\prime}_{\rm odd}C

(230)

Suppose
\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even}\geq\chi^{\prime}_{\rm odd}
. In that case, the last term in
Eq.

229
is negative (or zero), so we may bound

\displaystyle\lambda_{\rm other}\leq\frac{1}{2^{n}-1}\chi^{\prime}_{Z}+\frac{2}{2^{n}}\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even}.

(231)

On the other hand, suppose
\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even}<\chi^{\prime}_{\rm odd}
. In that case, we have that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\rm other}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2^{n}-1}\chi^{\prime}_{Z}+\chi^{\prime}_{\rm odd}\left(\frac{1}{2^{n-1}}-C\right)+C\chi^{\prime}_{\rm odd}

(232)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2^{n}-1}\chi^{\prime}_{Z}+\chi^{\prime}_{\rm odd}\frac{1}{2^{n-1}}.

(233)

Thus, we can combine the expressions and conclude that

\lambda_{\rm other}\leq\frac{1}{2^{n}-1}\chi^{\prime}_{Z}+\frac{1}{2^{n-1}}\max(\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even},\chi^{\prime}_{\rm odd}).

(234)

Finally, since
\chi^{\prime}_{0}+\chi^{\prime}_{Z}+\chi^{\prime}_{\rm even}+\chi^{\prime}_{\rm odd}=1
with each term in the sum non-negative, it immediately follows that no eigenvalue
\lambda_{\rm other}
can be larger than
2^{-n+1}
.
∎

Appendix D
QRAM is in the Clifford hierarchy

Here we show that for any
f
, the
n
-qubit QRAM gate
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}
from
Eq.

1
is in the
n
-th level of the logical Clifford hierarchy,
\mathcal{C}_{n}
from
Eq.

8
, and can therefore be teleported via the strategy sketched in
Section

2.2
. This can be seen directly as a consequence of Ref.
[
46
, Theorem 3]
, but here we give a self-contained proof.

We view the classical dataset
f
as an
n
-bit Boolean function
f\colon\{0,1\}^{n}\rightarrow\{0,1\}
, which we expand as a polynomal of its input bits over the field
\mathbb{F}_{2}
(i.e., addition and multiplication are done modulo 2). We consider all possible
2^{n}
monomials, denoted by
x^{e}
, where
e\in\{0,1\}^{n}
dictates the exponents of each bit

\displaystyle x^{e}=x_{1}^{e_{1}}x_{2}^{e_{2}}\cdots x_{n}^{e_{n}}\,.

(235)

Then, we may uniquely write

\displaystyle f(x_{1},\ldots,x_{n})=\bigoplus_{e\in\{0,1\}^{n}}c_{e}x^{e}

(236)

where
c_{e}\in\{0,1\}
is the binary coefficient for the monomial
x^{e}
, and
\oplus
denotes addition modulo 2. The degree of
f
is then given by the maximum degree of any monomial that appears in this expansion, that is,

\displaystyle\deg(f)=\max_{e\in\{0,1\}^{n}}c_{e}|e|\,,

(237)

where
|e|=\sum_{i=1}^{n}e_{i}
is the Hamming weight of
e
.

We will prove by induction that
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}\in\mathcal{C}_{\deg(f)}
. As the base case, we observe that if
\deg(f)=1
, then there is a bit string
u\in\{0,1\}^{n}
for which
f(x)=\bigoplus_{i=1}^{n}u_{i}x_{i}
. Thus, we have that
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}=\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}^{u}
, where

\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}^{u}
denotes the (logical) Pauli operator with
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}
in positions where
u_{i}=1
and
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$\mathbb{I}$}}\smash[t]{\mathbb{I}}}}
in other positions. That is, for degree-1 functions
f
, the QRAM operation is a Pauli operator,
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}\in\mathcal{C}_{1}
.

Next, we assume for induction that for any degree-
(d-1)
function
g
, the gate
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}\in\mathcal{C}_{d-1}
. We consider a degree-
d
function
f
, and we would like to show that this implies
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}\in\mathcal{C}_{d}
. Note that
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}=\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}^{\dagger}
, and that the family of QRAM operations obeys the general composition rule

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(h)$}}\smash[t]{V(h)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(h)$}}\smash[t]{V(h)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(h)$}}\smash[t]{V(h)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(h)$}}\smash[t]{V(h)}}}=\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f\oplus h)$}}\smash[t]{V(f\oplus h)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f\oplus h)$}}\smash[t]{V(f\oplus h)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f\oplus h)$}}\smash[t]{V(f\oplus h)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f\oplus h)$}}\smash[t]{V(f\oplus h)}}}

(238)

for any pair of
n
-bit Boolean functions
f,h
, where
f\oplus h
is the function for which
(f\oplus h)(x)=f(x)\oplus h(x)
. Thus, based on the decomposition in
Eq.

236
, we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}=\prod_{e\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}^{c_{e}}

(239)

Consider a factor
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}
associated with a degree-
|e|
monomial
x^{e}
. For any
X
-type Pauli string
X^{m}
, we compute

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}^{m}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}=\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V((x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V((x\oplus m)^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V((x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V((x\oplus m)^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V((x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V((x\oplus m)^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V((x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V((x\oplus m)^{e})}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}^{m}\,,

(240)

and thus

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}^{m}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}=\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V((x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V((x\oplus m)^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V((x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V((x\oplus m)^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V((x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V((x\oplus m)^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V((x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V((x\oplus m)^{e})}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}^{m}=\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}^{m}\,.

(241)

The crucial observation is that for any fixed
m
, the Boolean function
x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e}
, viewed as a function of
x_{1},\ldots,x_{n}
, has degree at most
|e|-1
. We see this by inspection of the expression

\displaystyle x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e}=\big[x_{1}^{e_{1}}\cdots x_{n}^{e_{n}}\big]\oplus\big[(x_{1}\oplus m_{1})^{e_{1}}(x_{2}\oplus m_{2})^{e_{2}}\cdots(x_{n}\oplus m_{n})^{e_{n}}\big]\,,

(242)

noting that distributing the multiplication of the second term gives
2^{|e|}
terms, one of which has degree
|e|
and will cancel the first term. The rest of the terms have degree
|e|-1
or lower.
Since
f
is a sum of monomials as in
Eq.

236
and
V(f)
decomposes into commuting diagonal factors as
V(f)=\prod_{e\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e})}}}^{c_{e}}
from
Eq.

239
, we have

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}^{m}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}=\left[\prod_{e\in\{0,1\}^{n}}\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})$}}\smash[t]{V(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})}}}^{c_{e}}\right]\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}^{m}=\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}^{m}\,,

(243)

where
g
is a Boolean function satisfying
g(x)=\bigoplus_{e\in\{0,1\}^{n}}c_{e}(x^{e}\oplus(x\oplus m)^{e})
. Since
f
has degree
d
,
g
is the sum of degree
d-1
functions, and thus has degree at most
d-1
. By the inductive assumption
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}\in\mathcal{C}_{d-1}
, which implies that
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}^{m}\in\mathcal{C}_{d-1}
as well. Furthermore, since
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}
is diagonal, it is straightforward to see that
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}^{m}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}=\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}^{m}\in\mathcal{C}_{d-1}
for any
m
. Finally, since any Pauli string
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}
is proportional to
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}^{a}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}^{b}
for some
a,b\in\{0,1\}^{n}
, these facts together imply
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}^{a}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}^{b}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}=\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(g)$}}\smash[t]{V(g)}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$X$}}\smash[t]{X}}}^{m}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$Z$}}\smash[t]{Z}}}^{b}\in\mathcal{C}_{d-1}
; thus,
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}\,\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}\in\mathcal{C}_{d-1}
for any Pauli string
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$P$}}\smash[t]{P}}}
. By induction, we conclude that
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}\in\mathcal{C}_{\deg(f)}
for all
f
. Since the maximum degree of any
f
is
n
, we have
\mathchoice{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-1.99997pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}{\overline{\vphantom{\raisebox{-0.39993pt}{$V(f)$}}\smash[t]{V(f)}}}\in\mathcal{C}_{n}
.
